data-score=\"34\"></p><button type=\"button\" data-reset=\"34\">清空本章作答，重新自测</button><div class=\"question\" id=\"q-35-1\" data-question=\"34,0\"><p class=\"meta\">1. Inference / limitation</p><p><strong>What is the immediate significance of the founding deed?</strong></p><div class=\"options\"><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"0\">A. It proves who ordered the interference with Mara&#x27;s ladder.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. It automatically makes Mara owner of both the theatre and the hospital.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. It permanently cancels every debt owed by the residents.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"3\">D. It supplies a specific basis for challenging the proposed transfer of the hospital rooms.</button></div><div class=\"feedback\" hidden=\"hidden\"></div></div></section><nav class=\"chapter-nav\" aria-label=\"Chapter navigation\"><button data-go=\"33\">← Previous</button><button data-go=\"35\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"v03c12","title":"12. The House That Kept Her Key","volume":"3","anchor":"chapter-0036","paragraphs":["On the morning Mercy House opened its second clinic room, Mara woke to the smell of toast and a furious argument about a doll. Pip believed the doll was a patient and entitled to breakfast. Gideon believed it had failed to complete the admission form. Agnes resolved the dispute by putting a small wooden plate beside the child's. \"A dangerous precedent,\" Cassian said, handing Mara a cup of coffee. \"Tomorrow the dragon will want a consultation.\"","He had slept in the guest room after the rooftop incident, though he insisted that the early records appointment was the reason. Mara did not challenge this explanation. His hair was untidy, his shirt was missing a button, and he looked considerably less like a duke than a man who had been made to hold a lamp while Ivo repaired a desk. She liked him so much in that moment that she spilled coffee on the table.","\"A review of my work?\" he asked.","\"The cup is defective.\"","\"Shall I investigate it?\"","\"Try not to imply that it comes from a superior bloodline.\"","His laugh reached Agnes at the serving hatch. She passed them another plate without comment, which Mara considered an act of considerable sensitivity. A moment later, a nurse asked Cassian to shift a sofa. He obeyed. The patients had begun to look to him for useful assistance rather than a speech, and he appeared to find the arrangement entirely agreeable.","The kitchen had a contract to supply lunch for a local workshop. Its income would pay for an extra shift. The grocer extended the credit account after seeing the first payment arrive. The guild of restorers had offered to help with the front arch. A girl from the neighborhood, whose first on-the-job training had consisted of carrying soup, now had a paid position assisting the cook. Mara held the signed offer as though its ink might vanish if she blinked.","Noor had news of her own. Mila's mother could continue her recovery at home with follow-up appointments, and her corrected pharmacy account was working. Mrs Bell had agreed on a plan for managing her illness while returning to some of the activities she enjoyed. Tomas still had gaps in his memory; he had also received confirmation that part of the route he drew matched the driving log. His account had become less lonely. None of these things required a miraculous cure to matter.","The committee gathered after breakfast. Agnes gave Mara a key tied with a yellow ribbon. It opened a small room above the theatre, where a repair bench stood beneath the window. There was also a bed. \"You keep leaving your coat here,\" Agnes said. \"We thought it would be easier if the coat had a room.\" Mara looked at the key, then at the faces around her. She had entered the house carrying an investigation order. She had never expected it to ask her to stay.","\"The rent?\" she managed.","\"Reduced in exchange for the workshop hours you requested. Written terms, because you would complain otherwise.\"","Rowan passed her the paper. \"An outrageously modest arrangement. I tried to persuade them you required a marble bath.\"","\"You may use the bucket,\" Agnes told him.","Mara's eyes threatened to become ridiculous. She saved herself by hugging Agnes, then Mrs Bell, then Pip, who immediately attempted to charge her for the doll's plate. Cassian watched from the doorway. When she reached him, he looked at the key in her hand and smiled in a way that made the room seem warmer. He did not turn the moment into a gift he had arranged. It belonged to the people who lived there.","At ten, they went to the harbor warehouse. Ivo carried a tool case in case any externally visible equipment needed identifying; June carried the copies she was permitted to hold. The appointed records officer joined them at the gate. The warehouse manager greeted the officer politely and the rest of them as though they had been delivered by mistake. A line of trucks stood along the fence. One carried sacks of fertilizer, another a painted cooling tower from a cancelled exhibition.","The manager offered a seat in a consulting room with no windows. Mara remained standing. From the door she could see a clerk wheeling three empty storage cases through the rear hall. One had the black square beneath a recently removed label. \"Those are hospital cases,\" she said. The manager glanced over his shoulder. \"We handle many medical clients.\" \"Then finding the right dispatch record should be an ordinary task.\"","He sent the clerk away. Cassian asked the officer to include the movement of the cases in the inspection note. The manager's smile acquired a small crack. He had expected either a wealthy purchaser or an angry amateur. The two people in front of him were proving annoyingly difficult to separate into those roles. Mara showed him the copied invoice. \"We are not here to value your entire business. Only this movement.\"","The dispatch record listed three sealed cases, an equipment trolley, and two accompanying boxes. A later attachment described the same consignment as maintenance stock. No patient was named. The changed description did not erase the hospital's ownership claim, but it complicated the next step. Mara asked to see the receiving signature. The manager said the original was in another building and would take a week to obtain.","A bell rang in the loading hall. The clerk returned with a tray of outgoing documents. Mara recognized the corner of a black-square label sticking from the bottom. The manager saw her looking and reached for the tray. She put the hospital invoice beside it, gently enough that he would have to move her hand to hide the matching marks. \"Here is an excellent opportunity to improve the estimated waiting time,\" she said.","He ordered the clerk to carry the tray into the private office. The officer stopped him. An employee in the hall began to shove a trolley across the doorway, blocking the view. Ivo caught its handle and turned it neatly aside. \"Mind the floor,\" he said. \"Fresh paint. You will ruin the wheels.\" His voice was mild; his grip was not. Cassian placed the copied request on the table and waited while the officer read the relevant line aloud.","The tray stayed. So did the original record. The cases had left the warehouse the previous evening and been transferred to a port operator for shipment. The agent's receipt carried a dock reference and the outline of a vessel name beneath a correction strip. Mara wanted to peel it back. Instead, she photographed the page as it was and asked for the earlier version. The manager suddenly had far more to say about clerical errors.","Outside, a man was hurrying toward a small car with a folder under his arm. The clerk whispered, \"That is the transport coordinator.\" The records officer called to him. He pretended not to hear. Cassian moved toward the car; Mara took the narrower path between two stationary trucks. The coordinator saw her appear ahead of him and stopped so quickly that the folder slid from his hand. A loose dispatch sheet caught in the wind.","Mara sprang onto the low step of a truck, caught the sheet against its side, and landed with her coat flaring around her. Cassian reached the driver's door before the man could open it. \"You have been asked a question,\" he said. It was the voice he used when people discovered that the courteous man and the investigator occupied the same body. The coordinator looked at Mara, then at the officer approaching behind them, and decided to hear properly.","The sheet showed a scheduled collection, not a voyage plan. The coordinator could name the port operator; he claimed not to know the final destination. Mara believed only as much as the document and his statement supported. It was still enough to move. The goods were no longer in the warehouse. The relevant record would now be at the dock, and any attempt to secure it would have to begin there. The officer called the port office while June made another copy.","Their car reached the outer harbor before the gate closed, but they were too late to inspect the loading area that day. A work stoppage had disrupted the schedule, and the cargo had been moved. Beyond the fence, a small white vessel lay in the distance with no readable name from where they stood. Mara gripped the copied invoice. After all the rooms, accounts, and careful questions, the next answer was waiting on water.","Ivo stood very still beside her. She remembered his fear and did not announce a heroic expedition on his behalf. \"We can start with the dock log,\" she said. He looked at her, grateful enough to hide it behind irritation. \"A radical proposal. Looking before leaping.\" Cassian adjusted the telescope they had borrowed from the theatre's scenery store. \"We shall have to acquire a better lens.\" \"And a less conspicuous coat for you,\" Mara said. \"The cranes are beginning to bow.\"","They went home before dark. At Mercy House, the repaired arch had its H again. The new screw gleamed against the old brass. Gideon had composed a short opening tune, which he played badly on purpose when Rowan attempted to sing. The kitchen served delicious soup, and a patient who had once eaten alone argued cheerfully about its seasoning. A house that had been treated as empty was making an enviable amount of noise.","Mara set the warehouse receipt beside the first stolen transfer form. The trail now led from a port clinic through hospital accounts, theatrical deliveries, and a departing shipment. She could not follow all of it that night. The hospital would need its people tomorrow; the harbor would need its witnesses. Noor would remain with the patients. Rowan and June would protect the copied accounts. Ivo, after a long look at the map, said he was coming to inspect the machinery from shore.","Cassian found Mara at the workshop window. Her new key hung beside her mother's mechanical silver flower. \"Six tomorrow?\" he asked. She nodded. He touched the ribbon once, then let his hand settle at her waist. \"You have acquired a home.\" She looked at the busy yard below. \"It appears to have acquired me.\" He drew her closer, and she rested against him without pretending she was merely tired.","\"Are you frightened?\" he asked.","\"A little.\"","\"So am I.\"","The answer was more useful than an impossible promise. She turned her face toward him. For a moment, the words she might have said seemed very near. Then Pip shouted from the yard that the doll had swallowed a button and demanded the best doctor in the city. Mara laughed into Cassian's shoulder. They went downstairs together. The sea could keep its questions until morning."],"questions":[{"type":"Inference / next step","stem":"Why will the next stage of the inquiry begin at the dock rather than remain at the warehouse?","options":["The patients have agreed that investigation at the hospital is no longer necessary.","The investigators have already confirmed the contents of the sealed cases.","The warehouse documents establish the final overseas destination of the goods.","The dispatch records place the goods beyond the warehouse and identify a port operator responsible for the next movement."],"answer":3,"explanation":"记录证明货物已离仓并交给港口经营方，下一份相关资料在码头。收货地点与运输记录不等于已知最终航程或箱内物品，住户也未作题干中那种表态。","evidence":[22,25],"shuffled":true}],"wordCount":1818,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0036\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">3</div><h1>12. Mara <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-4738\" data-words=\"4738\">laughed</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-4503\" data-words=\"4503\">into</a> Cassian&#x27;s <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-7522\" data-words=\"7522\">shoulder</a>. <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-8445\" data-words=\"8445\">They</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-3623\" data-words=\"3623\">went</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-2554\" data-words=\"2554\">downstairs</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-8552\" data-words=\"8552\">together</a>. <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">The</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-7328\" data-words=\"7328\">sea</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-1941\" data-words=\"1941\">could</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-4635\" data-words=\"4635\">keep</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-4571\" data-words=\"4571,4568\">its</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-6677\" data-words=\"6677\">questions</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-8904\" data-words=\"8904\">until</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-5343\" data-words=\"5343\">morning</a>.</p><section class=\"exercise\"><h2>Reading Practice</h2><p class=\"meta\">先完成选择，再查看解析。练习内容不计入词汇正文覆盖。</p><p class=\"meta\" data-score=\"35\"></p><button type=\"button\" data-reset=\"35\">清空本章作答，重新自测</button><div class=\"question\" id=\"q-36-1\" data-question=\"35,0\"><p class=\"meta\">1. Inference / next step</p><p><strong>Why will the next stage of the inquiry begin at the dock rather than remain at the warehouse?</strong></p><div class=\"options\"><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"0\">A. The patients have agreed that investigation at the hospital is no longer necessary.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. The investigators have already confirmed the contents of the sealed cases.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. The warehouse documents establish the final overseas destination of the goods.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"3\">D. The dispatch records place the goods beyond the warehouse and identify a port operator responsible for the next movement.</button></div><div class=\"feedback\" hidden=\"hidden\"></div></div></section><nav class=\"chapter-nav\" aria-label=\"Chapter navigation\"><button data-go=\"34\">← Previous</button><button data-go=\"36\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"v04c01","title":"The Price of a Quiet Harbor","volume":"卷四｜盐与钢的王冠","anchor":"chapter-0037","paragraphs":["At six o'clock the harbor was quiet enough for Mara to hear a light bulb buzz above the ticket booth. That was her first warning. A working port ought to have been a disorderly mixture of machinery, impatient drivers, and sailors who thought a whisper was a personal failure. Beyond the railway station, cranes stood stationary against a silver sea. The railroad carried no freight. A tug sounded its horn once, as if embarrassed by the echo, and fell silent.","Cassian stopped beside her at the gate. He wore an old overcoat instead of the grand black one the newspapers preferred. The disguise would have been more convincing if he had not looked like the owner of every skyscraper on the bay.","\"You could try being less handsome,\" she said.","\"I will assess the financial consequences.\"","\"They might downscale your portrait on the currency.\"","\"They have never put my portrait on the currency.\"","\"A notable failure of the public sector.\"","He took the bundle she was carrying without interrupting her attempt to tie her scarf. Inside it lay the hospital's copied invoices, a telescope with a scratched lens, and a packet of bread the chef had pressed on them before dawn. A strawberry had escaped from its paper wrapping. Cassian collected it and gave it to her with a contemplative expression.","\"For free?\" she asked.","\"I reserve the right to negotiate later.\"","The quiet did not mean the strike was universally supported. At the gate, elected workers' representatives were dealing with three separate disputes: unpaid wages, a proposed withdrawal of pension contributions, and the manufacturer's refusal to disclose what its sealed cargo contained. Although the company had described all three as resistance to modern machinery, only the last concerned the new equipment. To generalize from one dispute to the others would make the company's presentation simpler. It would also make it wrong.","A woman in an orange helmet watched Mara read the notices. Her name badge said LEAH NORTH, OPERATOR. Beneath it somebody had added TROUBLEMAKER in pencil.","\"Your predecessor called me that,\" Leah said when she noticed Mara looking. \"I decided to keep it. Saves the hostess having to introduce me at a party.\"","\"We are looking for a shipment that left Mercy House three evenings ago.\"","\"So is half the town. The other half has an investment in nobody finding it.\"","Mara showed her the hospital invoice. Leah's fist tightened around the paper. The invoice recorded a small medical lease. The dock log recorded an industrial generator, a restricted telecom unit, and nineteen sealed cells. One label claimed the contents were spare parts for a nuclear station overseas. Another said they were merchandise for a television company. The market value printed on the insurance form exceeded the hospital's annual income.","\"An extremely versatile generator,\" Mara observed.","\"It could yet become a robot for a TV show,\" Leah said. \"Last month they called the same model a potato sorter.\"","\"Does it sort potatoes?\"","\"If you feed it enough money, perhaps.\"","The joke faded when Leah unfolded a chart. A narrow curve climbed across the page: the number of damaged cargo seals recorded at each shift. A second curve showed the number of employees who had requested medical leave. Mara's first impression was that the lines rose together. The relationship was alarming, but the chart alone could not determine its cause.","\"Who prepared this?\"","\"My brother. He used to do data analysis for the company. They dismissed him for a non-standard recording procedure.\"","\"Was his procedure unreliable?\"","\"He wrote down what he saw.\"","That answer did not establish that his figures were correct. Mara asked for the original log. Leah studied her for a moment, then nodded. Respect, Mara thought, could consist of asking the question a frightened person least wanted to hear, provided one was willing to listen to the answer.","They crossed a crowded canteen in which nobody was eating. Men in oil-stained trousers sat beside a talkative linguist hired to translate the overseas operators' manuals. Two young workers watched a silent movie on a laptop. On its screen a penguin pursued a grape across an ice-covered balcony. The absurd little chase made the absence of ordinary dock noise more vivid.","Above the counter, a paid media advertisement announced a new profit-making partnership. It promised to industrialize the city's future, accelerate economic activity, and create high quality employment. A handsome golden logo depicted an atom enclosed by a wave. Beneath it, the small print described certain auxiliary services as exempt from service tax under an amendment to the tax code.","\"A magician could not fabricate a better diversion,\" Cassian said.","\"Magic requires people to pay attention to the right thing,\" Mara replied. \"This requires them to neglect it.\"","In the operator's office, Leah unlocked a cabinet. The original log was made of paper. Its fiscal entries had been copied from an online system, but her brother had added mechanical faults individually, in the fringe of each page. A valve that would not close. A cell whose protective casing had begun to decay. A current strong enough to disable a helmet lamp. An electric shock that a supervisor had described as innate clumsiness.","Mara traced the dates. The operator had not merely compared different workers at one moment. He had followed specific machines over time. That made the evidence harder to dismiss, although changes in reporting could still have affected the figures. Noor would need to reassess them before anyone could formally connect the machinery with the illnesses.","\"They told us the difficulty was a shifting baseline,\" Leah said. \"Then they told us there was no difficulty. Then they told us our concern might endanger the port.\"","\"Did the baseline shift?\" Mara asked.","Leah gave a merciless little smile. \"The machines did. The company moved the damaged ones to another station and started the series of inspections again.\"","Cassian photographed the pages. Mara found a thin, shiny residue beneath the cabinet handle. It was not dust. She touched it with a clean strip of paper and held the strip towards the torch Leah offered. The material caught the light like a fragment of snake skin.","\"Collodion,\" Mara said. \"Or something designed to resemble it. We use a similar film in restoration. You can lift a print without taking the surface beneath it.\"","\"Can you tell whose print?\" Leah asked.","\"No. And a residue this commonly used cannot identify a culprit. It may tell us how somebody copied the key.\"","Behind them a telephone began to dial itself.","Leah went still. The old instrument had no automatic dialer. Its round dial turned once, returned, and turned again. On the desk, the office laptop woke. A string of figures flowed across the screen; the keyboard produced a monotonous clicking noise although nobody touched it.","Cassian pulled the cable from the wall. The screen went black. The telephone rang.","\"That ought to be impossible,\" Leah whispered.","\"The line is separate,\" Mara said. \"We should assess the hardware before we assess the supernatural.\"","But her throat had tightened. There was a subtle difference between saying one did not believe in a fairy tale and finding oneself inside somebody else's fiction.","Cassian answered the telephone. He did not speak. For a moment his face became as calm and inflexible as the stern of a ship cutting through a cold sea. Then he set down the receiver.","\"We have four minutes,\" he said. \"They intend to collect the original log.\"","Leah looked towards the door. \"There are nineteen people between this office and the gate.\"","\"There are nineteen people they can blame,\" Mara said. \"That is different.\"","She opened her pocketbook and removed the little restoration kit she had brought aboard every investigation since the academy. A coil of fine wire, a nickel coin, adhesive, a blade. Tools with no impressive market value and plenty of potential.","\"Distribute the copies,\" she told Leah. \"Do it quietly. Nobody needs to riskily contrive a heroic revolt. We need witnesses, not martyrs.\"","\"And the original?\"","Mara glanced at the office ceiling, where a mechanical ventilation grille hung above a dusty shelf. The solution was roundabout and required a climb she would not enjoy. She was occasionally reminded that young adulthood had not supplied the immunity to gravity she had been promised.","\"We are going to weave it into something they will think of as worthless.\"","Three minutes later, a security team entered the canteen. Its chief announced that the port had received a credible terrorism warning. All personal packets would be searched. Nobody was allowed to move on to the next station.","The warning might have been genuine. Its timing nevertheless justified scrutiny. When an authority invokes danger, Mara thought, the question is not simply whether danger exists. One must also ask what the authority intends to do in its name.","She stood beside Cassian while a guard searched her bag. He found the copied invoices, bread crumbs, a scratched telescope, and a bruised strawberry. Mara's expression was one of mild, unhelpful frustration.","\"No weapon,\" the guard reported.","\"A misconception,\" Cassian said, looking at her.","\"I pardon you,\" Mara said sweetly, \"because you are carrying lunch.\"","Above them, inside the noise-making ventilation system, the original log rested between two empty advertising panels. Leah had taped it beneath a poster about the innate honesty of the manufacturer.","Outside, a freight whistle sounded. The ship they were looking for was leaving the harbor."],"questions":[{"type":"推断","stem":"What makes the residue on the copied key useful but insufficient evidence?","options":["It can suggest a copying method without uniquely identifying the person responsible.","It identifies the machine that caused every reported illness.","It shows that the original maintenance log must be false.","It proves that the elected workers organized the copying."],"answer":0,"explanation":"残留物提示复制钥匙的方法，但这种材料用途常见，无法仅凭它确定作案者。区分“提供线索”与“证明身份”。","evidence":[39],"shuffled":true}],"wordCount":1573,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0037\"
